## 1 About this document 

### 1.1 Introduction 

<mark>As today’s companies strive to maintain their competitive edge, they are constantly looking to improve the way they are doing business. Keeping pace with technological advantages, thriving in a fiercely competitive global marketplace, responding to demands for highly customized products – these are just some of the seemingly overwhelming challenges that must be met to ensure your company’s success. At Jeeves, we realize that a powerful ERP system plays a vital role in this success. We have therefore developed Jeeves ERP to empower your organization to quickly and efficiently manage change, today and in the future.</mark> 

### 1.2 Product overview 

<mark>Welcome to Jeeves’ Product overview — an overview of the features and functionality in your Jeeves ERP. This document is a high-level overview of the major capabilities contained in the described version, and is intended for anyone who wishes to gain a general understanding of Jeeves ERP’s overall features and functionality. It is organized according to functional groups. The document does not assume that the reader has detailed knowledge of the current and overall functionality, but requires general business knowledge relative to the application.</mark> 

<mark>The Product overview presents the Jeeves ERP applications and components and highlights the key features and functions available. This offers you a concise overview of the business process they support and the benefits your company can gain from using Jeeves ERP.</mark> 

<mark>Please note that Jeeves Professional Services can provide additional functionality as part of the packaging for different industries and companies. Your Jeeves Professional Services representative can advise you of suitable additions for your company’s processes.</mark>

### 1.5 Document conventions 

<mark>Use of typographic conventions in documentation helps users locate and interpret information easily. The following guidelines present specific typographic conventions. This document enables you to establish and maintain a common understanding of various conventions used in the document. For simplicity and consistency, this document describes the method of performing tasks. Other conventions used in this document are listed in the following table.</mark> 

|**Representation**|**Description**|
|---|---|
|Bold text|Procedures, references to screens, dialog boxes and their<br>elements,file and folder names,commands and buttons.|
|Numbered list|Stepwise instructions.|
|Bulleted list|Sets of features or options used toperform an activity.|
|Note text|Anyexception,extra information,orpitfalls.|
|Italic text|References to related documents.|

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0013-01.png)


## 2 Jeeves ERP’s User interface 

<mark>Jeeves ERP has a modern and attractive user interface. For new installations and upgrades, the Desktop client is the standard and only supported client.</mark> 

<mark>Existing Classic client installations remain supported under the support policy applicable to their release, which is why Classic client functionality is still described in this document.</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves ERP also offers a mobile client, a framework for designing mobile routines, and a range of standard mobile routines.</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves’ role based user interface can hide irrelevant information and enhance features that are important to a particular role. This helps you as a user to:</mark> 

- <mark>understand the information presented</mark> 

- <mark>focus on the right things and prioritize</mark> 

- <mark>reduce time spent on searching for relevant and correct information</mark> 

### 2.1 Desktop client 

<mark>Jeeves Desktop client is the standard and only supported client for new installations and upgrades. It is an installed Windows client built on Windows Presentation Foundation (WPF), a .NET-based framework for building user interfaces. In supported existing installations, it can run in parallel with the Classic client.</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves Desktop client is an improvement on the Classic client, adding a modern, flat user interface and an enhanced user experience, including productivity features such as a dashboard and rolebased designs. It allows automatic rendering of your classic program layout. With the separate Design studio, you can further improve the designs, and publish them for specific roles. As it is a Desktop client, there are few limitations regarding integration and interaction with other Windows applications.</mark> 

<mark>The Desktop client's dashboard allows you to always have an overview of the work that needs to be done. You can discover and solve problems, and do so in a way that can be personalized for your own routines and needs. The dashboard is task-oriented and makes it easy to access and manage data related to your tasks. It also makes it easy to collaborate and share information with your colleagues.</mark> 

<mark>The program menu, forms, and lists presented can also be role-specific and, if you have several roles in the company, you can switch roles on-the-fly.</mark> 

<mark>You can have any number of programs open at the same time. The open programs' icons are colorcoded to show their current mode (locked or opened for new entry or change). The navigation panel shows exactly where you are in the system. It is also clickable, which means you can revert to any previous level.</mark> 

<mark>There are filters for filtering programs in the menu, and both quick and advanced filters for filtering data in program lists.</mark> 

<mark>You can print reports connected to the open program from the Desktop client, including cloud compliant reports, both Jeeves ERP standard reports and reports designed using the report designer in Design studio.</mark> 

<mark>The Jeeves ERP standard dashboard has pre-built dashboards that expand the available ways for users to present and interact with information. The data in the Jeeves ERP standard dashboard is retrieved from the new ERP Server (Cloud) and can be viewed on the following on the Desktop client:</mark> 

   - <mark>Sales dashboard provides a visual representation of sales data such as open invoices, sales against forecast, sales by item group, etc.</mark> 

   - <mark>CFO dashboard provides a visual representation of financial data such as due customer and supplier invoices, performance overview, profit and loss, balance sheet, revenue, cash flow, etc.</mark>

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0014-01.png)


<mark>The Desktop client’s forms and lists are designed using the Design studio as described in the next section. There are, however, some settings you can make directly from the user client, such as:</mark> 

- <mark>Sort a program list or embedded list by clicking on one or more column header(s) and save the sort order</mark> 

- <mark>Save search conditions for re-use and translate them</mark> 

- <mark>Clone programs</mark> 

- <mark>Define intellisense fields</mark> 

- <mark>Define language, format, and print format settings</mark> 

- <mark>Set a list as a tree view</mark> 

- <mark>Copy values from previous entries to the current record</mark> 

<mark>There is also basic functionality for administrators and consultants, for example a function to update database tables from the user client.</mark> 

<mark>You can flag records and attach comments to them directly from the Desktop client. The dialog for managing flagged records, called Assign flagged record, lets you assign a flagged record to another user by selecting them from a drop-down list of valid users. Assigning a record to another user resets the flag color and makes the record appear as flagged for the selected user. Comments are displayed in the assigned user's Tagged records widget on the dashboard. A menu option called View flagged record info gives access to detailed information about flagged records, including comments, sender and receiver details, timestamps, and administrative functions.</mark> 

<mark>It is possible to configure different shortcuts to different servers with the same installation of the Desktop client.</mark> 

<mark>Much of the functionality in the user client is common to both the Desktop client and the Classic client and is therefore described in common sections further on in this chapter.</mark> 

### 2.2 Jeeves Launchpad 

<mark>Jeeves Launchpad is a dynamic loader and launcher for the Desktop client and Design studio. Cloud customers receive automatic upgrades through Launchpad, ensuring they always run the latest version.</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves Launchpad supports downloading, unpacking, and starting specific versions of the Desktop client and Design studio from remote or local sources, enabling flexible multi-environment setups.</mark> 

<mark>Key capabilities include automated version management with controlled upgrades, configurable startup options, multi-environment and shortcut support, visual progress indicators, and comprehensive process management.</mark> 

<mark>Platform components are delivered in a modular way, allowing certain corrections and updates to be applied through client updates without requiring full system upgrades.</mark> 

### 2.3 Design studio for Desktop client and mobile clients 

<mark>In Jeeves Design studio, you can create program designs "from scratch" or create new designs based on existing ones by saving them under new names. You can save designs to file, which means they can be distributed between users and different databases, both for review and for use.</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves Design studio supports the WYSIWYG concept (what you see is what you get), which implies that the designs you create here accurately reflect the appearance of the finished design in the Desktop or Mobile client.</mark> 

<mark>When creating new or changing existing designs you simply:</mark> 

- <mark>drag and drop new design elements into your design</mark> 

- <mark>drag and drop existing design elements within the design</mark> 

- <mark>use the Delete key to delete design elements</mark> 

<mark>1</mark> 4 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0015-01.png)


<mark>In Jeeves Design studio, you can see the design take form as you work on it to help you more easily create better designs.</mark> 

<mark>The Design studio includes:</mark> 

- <mark>Desktop client design</mark> 

- <mark>Mobile client design</mark> 

- <mark>Design of charts (pie, column, drill-down and line charts) including charts in program designs</mark> 

- • <mark>Design of dashboard widgets such as text widgets, chart widgets (including spline, stacked column and horizontal bar chart widgets), list widgets, web widgets and standard widgets such as the To-do widget, the Favorite programs widget, and the Tagged records widget</mark> 

- <mark>Design of the Macros tab dashboards</mark> 

- <mark>Design of the program menu tree</mark> 

- <mark>Smart tools like design outline and a zoom feature</mark> 

- <mark>Layout control options for forms and lists. You can set the orientation of a design to either vertical (default) or horizontal, select left or right placement of a form, and define the width of a list in pixels.</mark> 

- <mark>Extended configuration options for forms, lists, widgets, and tooltips, enabling clearer and more adaptable program designs.</mark> 

- <mark>Enhanced macro editing with improved suggestions and editing shortcuts.</mark> 

<mark>You can also have several designs ongoing at the same time and there is a built-in XAML editor that has been added for advanced users.</mark> 

<mark>Program designs originating from the Classic client can be imported into Design Studio and further developed, supporting reuse of existing configurations during modernization to the Desktop client.</mark> 

You can handle field settings in forms, lists and total windows from the Design studio, for example you can change font colors, automatically update lists, create own total fields, create, modify and translate tooltips for fields in forms, etc. You can handle own, custom terms/fields too. 

Several standard widgets have enhanced formatting and display options. The Text widget and Web browser widget support showing or hiding the widget title and heading, translating titles and headings to any supported language, and formatting text by font size, color, and alignment. 

The Card widget supports showing or hiding the widget title, changing prompt font and color, and defining value format. The Card text widget supports showing or hiding the widget title, displaying a sum value, defining numeric format, setting background color, and applying conditional formatting rules based on operator, value, and color. 

The Refresh option informs you if you have unsaved changes and gives you the option of discarding them and reloading the original design. 

The global search panel lets you search, open, edit and save designs, macros and connections, as well as create charts from statements. 

You can modify program menus in the tree format from the Design studio. You can add application programs and folders to the program menu tree design, move programs up or down in the program menu tree design, delete application programs and folders from the program menu tree design, set folder and application program properties, expand/collapse menu sections, save and publish the menu tree design, as well as export the menu to a program menu (.pmu) file. 

The Favorite programs widget displays the programs you have marked as favorites in the Desktop client and updates in real time. You can drag and drop it onto a dashboard in Design studio. The Tagged records widget displays flagged records in a procedure-list view on the Desktop client dashboard, giving users quick access to items they have flagged and commented on. It can likewise be placed on a layout using drag and drop in Design studio. 

<mark>1</mark> 5 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0016-01.png)


A new design is not applied until it is published, which implies that you can work on your design, gradually saving your changes, and publish it when it is ready. New designs must be saved before they can be published, but once published you do not need to republish them. When you publish the design, you can specify to whom and what the setting shall apply: 

- <mark>individual companies, company groups or all companies</mark> 

- <mark>individual users, user groups or all users</mark> 

- <mark>individual programs, all programs that share a window, or globally</mark> 

- <mark>specific client, either Desktop or Mobile, or both</mark> 

<mark>Any changes take effect automatically the next time the user(s) restart(s) or refreshes the user client.</mark> 

#### 2.3.1 Template editing in Design studio 

<mark>You can manage Jeeves report templates from Design studio. The Reports tab in the Developer menu contains a link to the Template manager dialog that allows you to view templates belonging to a selected report and company, create and edit report templates, add buttons and shortcut keys for printing a report with a certain template and delete report templates.</mark> 

<mark>An integrated report designer facilitates the replacement of Crystal Reports and SSRS reports with a cloud compliant format. The Report designer enables you to create new reports from scratch, bind them to data, and fully customize them. You can create new reports from a design context, open a report from site or file, save a report to site or to file (for export to another system), and publish/unpublish a report to a program or node/window context.</mark> 

#### 2.3.2 Macro editing in Design studio 

<mark>From the Developer tab in the Design studio you can develop new features in program designs, for example create new macros and create buttons to execute macros, which you can then place in your design. You can link/unlink from standard program events or field-specific events to macros, delete macros and create accelerator keys for functions. When saving or publishing a macro from the Macros tab, the macro code is displayed so that you can preview it before saving and publishing.</mark> 

The Macros tab shows a list of all program specific and global macros. The publish dimensions are indicated by icons. You can double-click on a macro to open it in a separate tab in the design area. You can select a macro and click the desired button on the Developer tab to perform the corresponding action from the program design/XAML tabs. 

The Macro links tab shows all connections related to the selected program and linked to global macros in Desktop and Mobile designs, linked events, buttons and hotkeys. A Field column displays the name of the field to which each macro is linked. The connections can be enabled/disabled via right-click. Here you can also view information about the program, node and window context publish dimensions. 

There are also tabs for viewing and modifying macro code and for removing connections. In the Macro Properties tab you can enter or modify the macro description and information. 

You can also initiate the Desktop client from the Design studio in order to run a macro for verification, which gives a seamless integration experience between the two. 

### 2.4 Mobile clients 

<mark>Jeeves ERP includes a framework for setting up mobile devices and users to be able to connect to the Jeeves ERP app and for designing mobile routines and processes to run from the app. Jeeves provides a suite of standard mobile routines as described further on.</mark> 

<mark>Mobile routines give users working out in the field, or off-site, access to relevant information in Jeeves, for example it gives service technicians access to their scheduled work, and makes it</mark> 

<mark>1</mark> 6 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0017-01.png)


<mark>possible for them to report back when work is completed. The connectivity to Jeeves ERP, while out in the field, or off-site, is complemented by typical mobile features, for example:</mark> 

- <mark>data entry using buttons, icons, drop-down lists, date/time spinners and switches</mark> 

- <mark>e-mails, text messages, phone calls, contacts and calendar access directly from Jeeves apps</mark> 

- <mark>maps and navigation, for example to be able to locate customer sites</mark> 

- <mark>the possibility to take photographs or videos and upload them to the server in order to record activities, meter readings and states, or to upload signatures to sign off work orders, etc.</mark> 

- <mark>the possibility to scan bar codes and QR (quick response) codes</mark> 

<mark>The Mobile clients are available for both iOS and Android platforms. You can configure your Jeeves ERP mobile app in an MDM (Mobile device management) provider like Microsoft InTune/MobileIron to configure REST URLs and profile names to deploy configurations using MDM.</mark> 

#### 2.4.1 Features 

<mark>Because there are special needs with regard to the size of the display and the behavior in mobile devices, there are many features that are specific to - or especially useful for - mobile clients. The following sections list out some of these features.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.1 Forms 

<mark>The basis of your design is a form with columns and tabs etc. (as in other clients). The difference is that in the mobile client, the form adapts to the available space, also when rotating the device.</mark> 

<mark>Detail records, for example, service order rows are displayed in the form of cards, giving a modern, compact look suitable for smaller displays.</mark> 

<mark>Certain controls, such as tabs, are collapsible and expandable.</mark> 

<mark>Program connections, report printouts, links to documents, saved searches, etc. can be accessed via a pop-menu:</mark> 

<mark>e-Mails, text messages, phone calls, contacts and the calendar can be accessed from the apps' forms. You can connect these actions to buttons or automatically trigger them on events, for example when closing a service order. You can have embedded browsers in the form for easy access to information.</mark> 

<mark>You can also build dialogs with multiple data entry fields, for example to enter or remove personal data.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.2 Lists 

<mark>When working with lists, you can click on the plus (+) icon to open a record in the Add mode. After making your changes, you can tap on the Done option to save your changes or on the Undo option to discard the changes.</mark> 

<mark>You can select/deselect multiple records from an embedded list in the Mobile client by long pressing and tapping on records to select. Once you have selected a records or records from the list, you can tap on an icon to perform the action associated with it. For example, to delete records, you can select the records you want to remove and tap on the floating Trash icon.</mark> 

<mark>For example, in an iPad you can have the search list to the left and form with embedded list etc. to the right.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.3 Check-off lists 

<mark>Many programs in Jeeves ERP are based on a special technique that we call checking off, which means that you select a set of transactions/records, for example search for all supplier invoices that are planned to be paid on a certain date, and then check off individual invoices to be included in the payment batch.</mark> 

<mark>In the mobile client, the windows that are designed for checking off have the following structure:</mark> 

<mark>1</mark> 7 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0018-01.png)


<mark>The top list corresponds to the left list in the classic client and presents the records that fulfill the criteria for processing (e.g. if you are checking off invoices for payment, all supplier invoices that are ready for payment) or a selected portion of them.</mark> 

<mark>You use the bottom list, which corresponds to the right list in the classic client, for selecting records for processing. You can then make any necessary adjustments to the records in the bottom list. When you are satisfied with the result in the bottom list you save it and, in many cases, run the next step in the process (e.g. journal, update or the like).</mark> 

<mark>A number of options are represented by icons in both the top and bottom list. Long press on a row to enable these options.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.4 Searches 

<mark>You can make quick, local searches within the current list, instead of searches being carried out on the server. Searches are run on all fields in the routine and not just on the values in one or a few specific field(s). Mobile client users can switch to Search mode to search in any field in the form as in the Classic and Desktop clients. You can also set up predefined searches, for example, as a technician using Jeeves’ Mobile Service routine automatically you can see a list of relevant service orders, without having to search.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.5 Intellisense 

<mark>You can use intellisense in fields while searching for data in related tables without having to jump to the table. You can enter the initial character(s) and click the Intellisense icon. A list of values which begin with the entered character(s) are displayed. You can use intellisense for fields like Item descriptions, company names, account names, etc.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.6 Notifications 

<mark>Notifications can be sent through a cloud based notification service with information and with a pre-defined action. For example, if a customer's address is changed, the server can send a notification and on clicking on the notification, the app can show the updated address along with the map.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.7 Buttons and icons 

<mark>You can connect buttons and icons to Jeeves' standard functions or own macros, and with special icons suitable for mobile interfaces.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.8 Map view 

<mark>You can use the map view, a mobile client specific design feature that displays a map, to for example show the customer's location.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.9 Date and time spinners 

<mark>You can use the date spinner connected to date fields or the time spinner connected to time fields to select dates and times.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.10 Diary entry 

<mark>You can make a diary entry when time reporting, for example a free text to be copied to the customer invoice.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.11 Images and videos 

<mark>You can pick an image or video from the gallery or take one with the device’s camera, or use the Signature control to sign off a record, and upload the image or video to the server. Images can be handled in various formats, such as .gif, .png, jpeg, or .bmp, and videos in .mov format.</mark> 

<mark>1</mark> 8 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0019-01.png)


##### 2.4.1.12 File sharing 

<mark>You can share a file (such as a document or an image) from a supported application with the Jeeves mobile app and then link that file to a record, e.g. to supplier invoices, customer orders, items etc. The Upload icon is visible when you open a record and one of more attachments are available for upload.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.13 Open attachments 

<mark>Attachments to records in Jeeves ERP can be opened in an app based on the document type (PDF, Excel, Word etc.). This works according to Jeeves' standard logic but can be used via macros too. When attempting to open an attachment, a menu is presented with the apps that are installed on the device and that support the relevant document format. (If no such apps are available no menu is displayed).</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.14 Reports 

<mark>Reports in Jeeves ERP can be opened in the routine from the Reports option in the Pop-up menu. The report is displayed in the format based on the settings in the Template editor. To view SSRS reports in the Mobile client, it must be set up and the Report services option must be selected.</mark> 

##### 2.4.1.15 Enter ID by scanning bar code 

<mark>If it has been set up, you can use the device's camera to scan a bar code, or a QR (quick response) code, instead of entering an ID manually or using search functionality. The mobile client automatically searches for the returned code (alphanumeric string). If a record in the list matches the scanned code, it is displayed.</mark> 

#### 2.4.2 Control session length 

<mark>It is possible to terminate the session after a certain period of inactivity, i.e. when the mobile client is closed without using the logout option, by configuring the idle time-out settings in the application pool.</mark> 

#### 2.4.3 Check logs 

<mark>Exceptions during calls made from the Mobile client/Jeeves server and error conditions are stored to a log file. The log file (for example, JvsRestLog 20160922 103426.txt, where 210160922 indicates the date on which the log file is created in the yyyymmdd format and 103426 to the time in the hhmmss format) is stored within the Logs folder on the REST machine.</mark> 

<mark>The logs are created when the client connects and disconnects from the client, server, or REST service.</mark> 

<mark>You can enable or disable the logging feature based on the app settings.</mark> 

#### 2.4.4 Macro functions and objects 

<mark>A number of macro functions and object methods have been created to support the mobile client.</mark> 

<mark>The SetXAMLProperty macro feature is available on the form level to customize components of the program (form and list). Currently, it is a mobile-specific function that only supports customizing UI elements. You can use it for many different purposes, for example to dynamically show/hide and change names on buttons, to keep the form in Add mode in programs used for only entering new records, to avoid having to pass via the program list, customize the program title, open/close sub nodes, customize a field, set the mode of the form, etc.</mark> 

<mark>The CreateSubTransaction macro feature is available on the form level to add/update/remove rows in an embedded list (child node).</mark> 

<mark>The LocationSubscription macro object provides location information of the client within the watch area.</mark> 

<mark>1</mark> 9 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0020-01.png)


<mark>The Navigate command allows you to change the source and destination properties of the MapViewer object. The Mobile UI changes to reflect the new route. If the source is empty, then mobile client will consider the current location as the source.</mark> 

<mark>The Request macro object allows you to call the REST API.</mark> 

<mark>The RestObject macro object allows you to construct a REST service request.</mark> 

#### 2.4.5 Standard routines in Mobile client 

<mark>Jeeves offers standard routines for some of the most common work flow steps that are suitable to be managed from a mobile device. These standard routines are intended to be maintained in Jeeves standard. They are delivered in a standard package of settings connected to a standard role and site groups created and maintained by Jeeves. Standard routines also use functionality developed as standard features, i.e. features developed to fulfill the requirements of a mobile routine that can also be used in Jeeves Desktop and Classic clients.</mark> 

<mark>The following standard mobile routines are available:</mark> 

- <mark>Jeeves Mobile order - for salespeople visiting customers to enter and confirm orders, also to search among and work on existing orders</mark> 

- <mark>Jeeves Mobile shopfloor - for users on the shop floor to report on own operations</mark> 

- <mark>Jeeves Mobile warehouse - for warehouse staff to report their work directly from where the work is performed</mark> 

- <mark>Jeeves Mobile field service – for service technicians to view and update service orders</mark> 

### 2.5 Classic client 

<mark>The Classic client is retained for existing installations covered by the support policy applicable to their release. It is no longer delivered for new installations or upgrades.</mark> 

<mark>The Classic client is Jeeves’ long-standing, Windows-based client. The designs in the newer Desktop client can be automatically generated with the same look as in the Classic client, if desired.</mark> 

#### 2.5.1 Data entry 

<mark>You can set up the first entry field in each form, copy selected values from the previous record. You can also choose between data entry in the form or the list.</mark> 

<mark>Specified values are validated directly when entering data.</mark> 

#### 2.5.2 Design 

<mark>You can install role-based tabs and frames but there is also a possibility to adapt them or create your own design. There are tools for :</mark> 

- <mark>changing headers in forms and lists</mark> 

- <mark>changing fonts and colors of fields</mark> 

- <mark>changing background color</mark> 

- <mark>changing size and position of forms and lists</mark> 

- <mark>hiding or showing programs, nodes, reports and terms</mark> 

- <mark>creating styles</mark> 

- <mark>defining formats for input and display of dates, time, numeric and monetary values</mark> 

<mark>and other functions.</mark> 

#### 2.5.3 Quick settings via right-click 

<mark>In addition to the tools on the</mark> **<mark>Tools</mark>** <mark>menu, there are a number of settings that can quickly be made by right-clicking on fields in forms. These settings are field-related and require that you focus or check the current field(s) before selecting a setting option. The settings that are available are:</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark>

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0021-01.png)


- <mark>Hide all flagged fields</mark> 

- <mark>Hide current field</mark> 

- <mark>Add help for current field</mark> 

- <mark>Settings for current field (add filter condition, activate automatic wildcards, change texts, fonts, colors of fields and make conditional formatting settings)</mark> 

- <mark>Show current settings</mark> 

- <mark>Tabs</mark> 

- <mark>Create total field from current list field</mark> 

<mark>Via right-click you can also create bookmarks regarding records and transactions in Jeeves ERP, for example, regarding a sales order, a customer, a general ledger account etc. This can be helpful if you need to make a note or if you need to ask or inform a colleague about something in the database. You can:</mark> 

- <mark>save bookmark to file</mark> 

- <mark>send bookmark as email</mark> 

- <mark>send bookmark with current conversation</mark> 

#### 2.5.4 Advanced settings 

<mark>Furthermore there are functions for advanced settings such as user-defined sorting and selections in reports, as well as user-defined right-clicks to programs.</mark> 

#### 2.5.5 Program clones 

<mark>Jeeves’ function for cloning programs lets you create customized entry forms while the standard program form remains intact for reference.</mark> 

#### 2.5.6 Program menus 

<mark>You can have any number of program menus and either make one or more menus visible or select menus from a list. A default menu can be set up per user or group, if required.</mark> 

#### 2.5.7 Role menus 

<mark>If Jeeves’ roles are used there are role-based menus according to role-based processes for distribution, manufacturing, supporting processes etc.</mark> 

#### 2.5.8 Program menu tools 

<mark>Jeeves ERP has built-in tools to adapt pre-defined role menus or create own program menus per user, group, language etc. Programs on a menu can have standard icons that represent the program type, or you can use own icons.</mark> 

#### 2.5.9 Favorites and last used programs, program aliases 

<mark>There is a Last used folder, in which Jeeves ERP automatically stores the last used programs. This can be useful if you need to retrace your steps for any reason. The folder is cleared when you log out. There is also an option for adding programs that you use regularly to a personal Favorites folder displayed above Jeeves’ program menu.</mark> 

<mark>There is an option for creating own program aliases or short-names for programs in Jeeves ERP. With the Run program... option you can run a program without searching for it on the menu if you know the program's internal data name in the system, or if you use an own alias, if you are authorized to do so.</mark> 

<mark>2</mark> 1 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0022-01.png)


#### 2.5.10 User defined program links 

<mark>Jeeves ERP standard has many built-in program links to make it easier to access and maintain related information without having to close the program you are using. These links are accessed via embedded lists, right-clicks or nodes. If you often need to access a certain program from another program but there is no link available in Jeeves, you can create your own links between programs.</mark> 

#### 2.5.11 Graphical tree structure 

<mark>To create an easier navigable interface, you can display the content in list forms as a tree structure. You can display all list forms in this way, but it is especially useful for BOM, packages, documents, etc., that comprise of several levels.</mark> 

#### 2.5.12 Business graphics 

<mark>In Jeeves ERP, there is a built-in function with which you can quickly create and display business graphics, such as diagrams and charts, from a number of selected records in the list and fields in the form. The function includes a guide for creating and adjusting the graphics. You can choose between three types of charts, line, bar or pie chart.</mark> 

<mark>You can print and copy graphics and save them to a number of different graphics formats or in PDF or HTML format. You can create macros to link graphics to forms for re-display.</mark> 

### 2.6 Setting dimensions and storage 

#### 2.6.1 Settings dimensions 

<mark>Settings can apply to:</mark> 

- <mark>individual companies, company groups or all companies</mark> 

- <mark>individual users, user groups or all users</mark> 

- <mark>a specific client (device) or all types of clients</mark> 

- <mark>individual programs, all programs that share a window or globally</mark> 

#### 2.6.2 Storage of settings 

<mark>Separate storage of settings ensures that they are not affected during upgrade. This also implies that several Jeeves ERP installations can share the same settings and that you can test installations with and without settings. You can even set up several different site repositories against the same Jeeves ERP installation, for example, if you want to test settings without affecting the installation.</mark> 

###### **Note** 

Most of the remaining sections in this chapter apply to the Desktop client and, for supported existing installations, the Classic client. 

### 2.7 Important, general features 

<mark>There are a few general features in Jeeves’ user interface that are important to learn as quickly as possible:</mark> 

- <mark>All programs in Jeeves ERP have at least two windows, a form window and a list window. Each form window has a list window with the same title directly linked to it. The list window displays a selection of records or all existing records in the table, while the form window displays one record at a time.</mark> 

- <mark>For the majority of business transactions Jeeves ERP creates a header and one or more details, or rows. Example: If you enter a customer order you create a header and one or more order details, i.e. order rows. The header contains data common to the whole order,</mark> 

> <mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

<mark>2</mark> 2 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0023-01.png)


<mark>for example, the customer company, delivery and payment terms etc. The rows specify the items and services etc. to be delivered on that order and any deviations from the conditions stated on the header. This structure means that the majority of programs for the entry of business transactions contain a form for the header and separate forms (or an embedded window) for the rows. If you exit the form or list for the rows you are normally automatically returned to the header.</mark> 

### 2.8 User friendliness 

#### 2.8.1 Keyboard shortcut commands 

<mark>Shortcut commands allow users to work with the keyboard instead of the mouse in many operations.</mark> 

#### 2.8.2 Check-off lists 

<mark>Many programs have lists that simplify data entry, for example, a list of order rows to be picked, invoices to be paid etc.</mark> 

#### 2.8.3 Buttons and shortcuts 

<mark>Many functions are run via buttons or shortcuts. You can also create your own buttons for recurrent searches, functions, and printouts.</mark> 

#### 2.8.4 Embed graphs and browsers in forms 

<mark>You can embed objects, such as PDF, web browsers, and graphs in Jeeves' forms. The function is primarily for displaying supplier invoices but is general and can be used for displaying other files.</mark> 

#### 2.8.5 Parallel programs 

<mark>You can run several programs in parallel. You can then toggle between them, copy data, etc.</mark> 

#### 2.8.6 Support for touch-enabled devices 

<mark>The Jeeves ERP UI supports single point touches and swipe gestures on touch-enabled devices. The touch keyboard is shown automatically when a text input field is in focus. The swipe gestures are available in the intellisense fields.</mark> 

#### 2.8.7 User profiles 

<mark>User profiles are available in Jeeves mobile routine to switch between different REST servers. Each user can have one or more unique profiles. The profile contains role, company and other user related details.</mark> 

### 2.9 Searching and retrieving information 

#### 2.9.1 Unlimited search possibilities 

<mark>One of Jeeves’ greatest strengths is that you can search on almost any field, with or without values. Examples of use: search transactions with no voucher number to find unposted transactions, or transactions with your signature, entered on a certain date and with a certain status.</mark> 

<mark>You can search on any part of the field content, for example, all companies whose names start with or contain one or more specific character(s). You can also use wildcards for unknown/unspecified characters. If there are several companies in the database you can search in multiple companies. There is a possibility to save an unlimited number of search filters.</mark>

Jeeves ERP’s User interface 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0024-01.png)


#### 2.9.2 Links to related programs 

<mark>In all programs you can view related information without passing via the program menu, for example, view customer or item data from orders. There is built-in intellisense in fields linked to other tables, for example, from order entry you can directly get a list of customers whose names start with a specific character without jumping to the customer program. You can even search against several fields (columns in the intellisense grid) simultaneously. You can define several fields (columns) to be displayed in the result list, for example, when searching with intellisense in the Company name field, you might also want the company ID and city/state to be displayed. There are also other features you will recognize from Jeeves' program lists and drop-down lists, such as the following:</mark> 

- <mark>You can type more initial characters to jump directly to certain records in the list (only in the first column).</mark> 

- <mark>You retrieve a row to the form with Space or with double-click.</mark> 

- <mark>You can zoom in and out with Ctrl and the scroll button on the mouse.</mark> 

#### 2.9.3 Drill-down 

<mark>You can drill-down to included or related transactions, for example, from an invoicing journal view the included invoices, from an invoice view the included order(s), from an order view picked order rows etc.</mark> 

#### 2.9.4 Save search in overlying level 

<mark>There is a function on the</mark> **<mark>Search</mark>** <mark>menu for saving a search from an underlying level to an overlying level, i.e. a search made in an embedded list, in a right-click program or in an underlying program node, to be saved for use in the main form. Example of usage: You display an order/purchase order, drill down to the item rows and search for a specific item. If at this time you want to search for all order/purchase order rows for the same item, you can use this function.</mark> 

#### 2.9.5 Retrieve previous search condition 

<mark>You can retrieve the last search condition used, for example, if you want to edit or add to it.</mark> 

### 2.10 Documentation 

#### 2.10.1 Context sensitive documentation and integrated manuals 

<mark>Jeeves ERP has comprehensive and context sensitive field, table and program documentation with hyperlinks to related chapters describing the setup and work flow(s), and other functionality.</mark> 

<mark>The documentation is web-based and public, which implies that you can use it immediately, without any installation or file copying. This also makes it possible for Jeeves to publish new and corrected documentation more directly.</mark> 

<mark>The web-based documentation has a fresh and modern interface with powerful searchability and filtering. Version management was introduced from Update 13, so you can select which Jeeves ERP version and Update you want to read about. You can also switch between English and Swedish onthe-fly.</mark> 

<mark>The documentation presents lists in the form of a grid, in which you can sort on and arrange the columns, as well as filter in them. You can search on one or more individual word(s) or on an exact phrase. You can use a wildcard for any number of unknown characters. You can also search and filter on the titles in the table of contents or make a free-text search.</mark> 

<mark>If you know the number of a chapter or the name of a field, program or table, you can enter it directly in the address (URL).</mark>

#### 2.10.2 Customer-specific tool tips 

<mark>There is also a built-in function to quickly enter own tool tips for fields and to translate tooltips if users run the system in different languages.</mark> 

### 2.11 Language versions 

#### 2.11.1 Language versions of the product 

<mark>Jeeves ERP can currently be provided in the following, maintained language versions:</mark> 

   - <mark>Swedish</mark> 

   - <mark>English</mark> 

   - <mark>Polish</mark> 

   - <mark>French</mark> 

   - <mark>German</mark> 

   - <mark>Norwegian</mark> 

   - <mark>Finnish</mark> 

   - <mark>Danish</mark> 

   - <mark>Dutch</mark> 

   - <mark>Simplified Chinese</mark> 

- <mark>Besides these, the following language versions exist in part or for certain, earlier Jeeves versions:</mark> 

   - <mark>Turkish</mark> 

   - <mark>Slovak</mark> 

   - <mark>Czech</mark> 

   - <mark>Russian</mark> 

   - <mark>Estonian</mark> 

   - <mark>Spanish</mark> 

   - <mark>Italian</mark> 

#### 2.11.2 Default language and language selection 

<mark>You can set up the client's default language per user, group etc. If desired, users can switch between available languages on-the-fly. This can even be achieved for data such as payment terms, chart of accounts and the like.</mark> 

### 2.12 Integration between applications 

<mark>If required, you can use all the integration between applications. This, together with the relational database technology, eliminates the need for duplicate entries. It is also easy to retrieve data from external systems or manually enter records in applications that are not included in the installation.</mark>

## 3 Print design and output management 

<mark>Jeeves ERP includes a large number of predefined documents and reports, such as delivery notes, order confirmations and payment reminders, as well as different types of statistic reports and journals. The documents and reports are linked to the programs to which they belong, i.e. delivery notes, order acknowledgements and invoices are found on the report menu for the various order entry and invoicing programs.</mark> 

<mark>The same document/report can have different appearances depending on the printout template. If there are several templates, they are shown in a submenu to the document/report.</mark> 

<mark>Because it is necessary to be able to customize printouts, Jeeves ERP also includes a design tool called the Template editor. The Template editor can be used to create one or more templates for a document or report and edit the appearance in different ways, such as:</mark> 

- <mark>move fields or other parts of the report</mark> 

- <mark>add and edit fixed texts</mark> 

- <mark>change size, font etc. for fields, parts of the report or the entire report</mark> 

- <mark>edit copies with different layouts</mark> 

- <mark>hide fields or parts of the report and</mark> 

- <mark>set up which printer is to be used for the template in question</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves ERP also offers several alternatives for producing your own reports, for example, an inhouse developed report generator as well as an external report generator.</mark> 

<mark>See also separate chapter on Jeeves Document management (EDM) describing, for example, that you can also print directly to EDM.</mark> 

### 3.1 Languages 

<mark>Template proposals are provided in several languages and Jeeves templates are provided in English and Swedish.</mark> 

### 3.2 Output management 

<mark>You print a report simply by searching for one or more records, for example, one or more orders, and then selecting the report program from the report menu and the desired print template.</mark> 

#### 3.2.1 Own report menus 

<mark>With the</mark> **<mark>Show selected reports</mark>** <mark>/</mark> **<mark>Show all reports</mark>** <mark>option you specify whether you only want to see the reports you have added to the report menu from the template editor or if you want to see all reports connected to the program.</mark> 

#### 3.2.2 Print format 

<mark>If desired, you can print to screen instead of to a printer. (You can also print to a printer from the Print to screen dialog.) You can also export the report to file in various formats, such as Excel, HTML, PDF, etc. and either save the file to disk or send it directly with an e-mail. You can define printout formats, separate from display formats, for dates, base currency, foreign currency and numeric terms. You can use the export functionality to store documents created in Jeeves, for example, to store customer invoices in PDF format. You can then use Jeeves Document management to link the invoice printout to the invoice record in the database. In this way, it is possible to meet the legal requirements that certain countries have regarding the electronic storage of original documents.</mark> 

<mark>The following are the setting priorities for printout formats:</mark> 

<mark>Priority for format settings of numeric values: 1) on the term and 2) in General settings, Print format.</mark> 

<mark>2</mark> 6 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Print design and output management 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0027-01.png)


<mark>Priority for format settings of dates: 1) on the term, 2) in General settings, Print format and 3) in the system defaults.</mark> 

<mark>Priority for currencies: 1) Currency format setting on base or foreign currency fields, the next priority is same as the priority settings for dates.</mark> 

#### 3.2.3 Report generators 

<mark>For information about the built-in report generator please refer to the chapter on</mark> _<mark>Jeeves General ledger</mark>_ <mark>.</mark> 

#### 3.2.4 Dynamic reports 

<mark>Jeeves has a built-in function for dynamically creating reports on a number of selected records in the list and fields in the form. You simply search for the desired records, check the required fields in the form and start the report. The dynamic report is printed to screen.</mark> 

<mark>You can change the order and the width of the columns by dragging and dropping the columns and the lines between them. You can also right-click on a column, which opens a pop-up menu with modification options such as sum, alignment and change of font.</mark> 

<mark>You can export reports from the preview in a number of different formats, such as PDF, HTML, RTF, Excel, Text format, JPG, etc. You can e-mail reports directly from the preview. You can also save reports and add them to the report menu in the appropriate program.</mark> 

#### 3.2.5 Link report to button/shortcut 

<mark>You can create a button for printing with a certain template. Users can then start printing simply by pressing the button or entering a shortcut command.</mark> 

#### 3.2.6 Schedule recurring printouts/updates etc. 

<mark>There is a possibility to schedule print and update jobs. If a company has very large transaction volumes it can be an advantage to schedule certain processes so that they are run automatically, for example, at a set time each night. There are also processes that must be run at predetermined time intervals, and for these it is an advantage to be able to schedule the process.</mark> 

#### 3.2.7 <mark>Cloud printing service</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves Cloud printing service lets you print to physical printers as well as to virtual printers such as PDF and Excel. A Cloud agents program provides an internal registry of the cloud agents used in cloud printing. The Physical devices program supports physical and virtual printer mapping, device availability, and logical device configuration. You can redirect direct print jobs to a different printer and reassign print jobs to other cloud agents if one is unavailable. Jeeves returns full printer and job metadata in JSON format, including queue assignment, printers, report and publication IDs, parameters, and file path for virtual printer output. When a print job completes, its parameters are stored in a Job parameter history table, where you can view the parameters used for each job in an embedded list.</mark> 

#### 3.2.8 XML adapter (powered by StreamServe) 

<mark>Jeeves ERP includes functionality for printing XML files based on reports in Jeeves. All fields that are available in the report template are also available in the XML file. The option to print XML files can be set for any combination of report, template and user.</mark> 

<mark>Jeeves’ StreamServe template package includes the most used documents for an ordinary distribution or manufacturing company. The templates are delivered in Swedish and English as a part of the StreamServe base package for Jeeves ERP. There is also a tool for converting your Jeeves templates to StreamServe templates.</mark>

Print design and output management 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0028-01.png)


#### 3.2.9 Separate report execution from printout 

<mark>Report execution can be separated from printout if required. When you run a report, you have the option to have it execute in the background and not generate a printout. For example, you can create invoices and print them out later.</mark> 

<mark>For all reports that are associated with a template, you have a no printout option that allows you to run the report without generating a printout.</mark> 

#### 3.2.10 Reporting Services (SSRS) 

<mark>SQL server reporting services (SSRS), which is part of our technology stack, has matured enough to be an alternative for Crystal reports.</mark> 

<mark>SQL Server Reporting Services (SSRS) is a generic part of SQL Server, with a distributable development tool included in the license. SSRS reports can be viewed in an embedded web browser thereby not demanding client installations. SSRS also adds distribution and subscription possibilities.</mark> 

<mark>You can use SSRS as a tool for creating new reports separate from the current Jeeves reports.</mark> 

<mark>You can manage SSRS reports from the Classic, Desktop and Mobile clients.</mark> 

#### 3.2.11 Print to EDM 

<mark>A selection of reports can be printed directly to EDM. On selecting the report from Jeeves, the report is generated in the desired format and automatically linked to the record. You can then view and print the report as and when needed.</mark> 

### 3.3 Print design 

<mark>You can have any number of templates per report/document, for example, different invoice templates linked to different customers.</mark> 

#### 3.3.1 Company adaptation of templates 

<mark>So-called overlaid templates can be used for global information, i.e. information that is to be included in several templates. One such example is own company’s address data to be added as a footer to all external documents. You can paste images such as company logos, and also add fields.</mark> 

#### 3.3.2 Layout 

<mark>You can select font, size, frames etc. for fields, for a part of a report or for a whole report. There are also functions for:</mark> 

- <mark>Set up printer with the possibility to link different report templates to different printers</mark> 

- <mark>Write a fixed text</mark> 

- <mark>Add a picture/copy</mark> 

- <mark>Snap to grid</mark> 

- <mark>Hide/show edit marks and details</mark> 

#### 3.3.3 Link a template to a company, country or language 

<mark>A print template can be linked to a company, a country or a language etc. in the system. This is limited to certain external documents and reports. The template is then automatically selected according to the print rule.</mark>

Jeeves ERP CRM 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0029-01.png)


## 4 Jeeves ERP CRM 

<mark>Jeeves CRM is a highly functional CRM system including contact, sales and campaign management. It is fully integrated with all other applications in Jeeves ERP, which means it has direct access to all transaction data in the ERP system, such as open orders, accounts receivable, service orders etc. Its integration with Jeeves Document management gives direct access to all relevant documents regarding customer relations, like e-mails, contracts or quotations. All in all, this gives a complete picture of your customer relations.</mark> 

### 4.1 Grouping and classification 

<mark>The module provides many options for individual groupings and classifications of companies, business opportunities and contacts. This may include geographical groupings, relationships, sectors, product information, distribution data, etc.</mark> 

### 4.2 Companies and prospective customers 

<mark>In Jeeves ERP you have a common Company table for company information regardless of the type of company – prospective customers and business opportunities, customers, carriers and suppliers. This eliminates the need for duplicate entries and maintenance of company information.</mark> 

<mark>Additional, CRM-specific information is managed in related tables. Here you can handle all types of customers - domestic and international, customers with another payer, with factoring, with several points of delivery etc.</mark> 

#### 4.2.1 Language management 

<mark>You can manage the language of item names, units, payment terms, delivery terms, delivery methods etc. and print external documents such as quotations in the customer's language.</mark> 

### 4.3 Relationship management 

<mark>Relationship management includes functions for maintenance of information on companies, their contact persons and contacts and relationships with these.</mark> 

#### 4.3.1 Company relations 

<mark>There are extensive possibilities for own segmentation/classification of companies, own keywords, gradual build-up of information about business relationships, planning and reporting of activities, customer cards etc.</mark> 

#### 4.3.2 Contact relations 

<mark>You can enter data about contacts in a company in connection with a new entry of a company or do so afterwards.</mark> 

<mark>There are extensive possibilities for own classification of contacts at companies, own keywords, contact information, direct mail via button etc.</mark> 

#### 4.3.3 Planned and reported activities 

<mark>You can plan and report activities at both company and contact level. Activities entered at contact level will automatically be available at company level.</mark> 

### 4.4 Sales support 

<mark>Here there are functions for entering and processing business opportunities (for instance created in contacts with companies in connection with different marketing activities) according to an own predefined sales process. By changing the status code the probability and sales phase can be</mark> 

<mark>2</mark> 9 ( <mark>215)</mark> 

<mark>© Copyright Forterro Sweden AB. All Rights Reserved.</mark> 

Jeeves ERP CRM 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0030-01.png)


<mark>automatically changed, planned activities can be generated or the opportunity can be set to won or lost. In addition, there are functions for processing data about possible and probable sales and margins, quotation management, pipeline analysis and forecasting.</mark> 

#### 4.4.1 Business opportunities 

<mark>There are functions in Jeeves CRM for entering and following up on various types of business opportunities. The functions can be used to quickly enter all business opportunities that have been created, for example, during marketing activities such as fairs, telemarketing campaigns, etc. in order to divide them up among sales personnel to be followed up. They can also be used for entering and following up on negotiations with new or existing customers.</mark> 

<mark>You can enter one or more business opportunities for a company. If you want to group a company's business opportunities, you do so by entering a project and linking the company's business opportunities to the project. Linking to a project also enables cost follow-up for the sales process.</mark> 

<mark>It is possible to enter business opportunities without specifying a company since the information may be sensitive at this early stage.</mark> 

<mark>All historical data for closed business opportunities is available and can be searched and analyzed.</mark> 

#### 4.4.2 Probable sales 

<mark>Here there is functionality to make it easier to enter and produce possible or probable sales figures and margins, both in total and your own share (your own company's share or the salesperson's share) and also in base currency or foreign currency according to the specified exchange rate. You can search in the fields for fiscal year and/or period and view totals for the selected period in the list.</mark> 

#### 4.4.3 Sales process 

<mark>There is also an option for automation of certain status changes, for example, when changing to status Refused, the probability can be changed to 0 and the business opportunity can be changed to Lost, whereupon it is automatically moved to the Business opportunities, history program. In addition, the deviation from the original or estimated decision date is calculated.</mark> 

#### 4.4.4 Quotations 

<mark>In the Quotations node, you can see all quotations linked to the business opportunity. There is also a field for a quotation ID in the main form where you can specify the relevant quotation, if required.</mark> 

#### 4.4.5 Analysis 

<mark>In the Pipeline analysis node, you can see a graphical pipeline analysis showing the number of records and sum of possible sales value for each sales phase. The graph shows all active business opportunities, i.e. connected to a sales phase.</mark> 

<mark>In the Future income node you can see future income, one year ahead, based on possible sales value for your business opportunities, grouped by either period or quarter. The graph includes all active business opportunities, i.e. connected to a sales phase.</mark> 

#### 4.4.6 Activities 

<mark>There are many alternatives you can choose between when you plan and report marketing activities. You can choose between various programs. You can select the level to which you want to link an activity, i.e. to a company, to a business opportunity or to a contact person. The activities follow the company independent of the current business relationship.</mark> 

<mark>If you want a detailed follow-up on times and costs, use the integration with the Time, projects, assignments module and, for example, copy programs from that module. If you want costs/revenues to affect the reporting, the project must also be invoiced in that module.</mark>

Jeeves ERP CRM 


![](images/Jeeves_ERP_2026.Q2.2_Product_overview.pdf-0031-01.png)


#### 4.4.7 To-do list with Outlook link 

<mark>There is also a program for searching among own planned activities, for re-planning and reporting, which is linked to Jeeves’ internal calendar. The program also has a function for copying planned activities to the personal Outlook calendar including details about the customer, quotation, opportunity and contact person the activity is related to. The personal Outlook calendar is also embedded in the program in order to facilitate planning.</mark> 

#### 4.4.8 Statistics, credit information and other customer information 

<mark>As the CRM module is integrated with other modules in Jeeves ERP, you can bring up information created in other modules, for example, information on sales, ledger balances, etc. to get a better overall picture of your customers.</mark> 

### 4.5 Marketing support 

<mark>There are functions for setting up campaigns and distribution lists and you can use Jeeves' unlimited search capabilities to make selections of the contacts to be included in the campaign/distribution list. There are also functions to facilitate entry of campaign responses and to follow up the outcome.</mark> 

<mark>Campaigns can also be linked to projects for follow-up on times, incomes and costs.</mark> 

#### 4.5.1 Campaigns and mailings 

<mark>There is a program for definition of distribution lists for use in campaigns, etc. with information about the companies and any associated contacts the campaign is aimed at.</mark> 

<mark>A campaign (a distribution list) can be created in many different ways:</mark> 

- <mark>You can create a campaign for a number of companies in the Company marketing program.</mark> 

- <mark>You can create a campaign for a number of contacts in the Contacts program.</mark> 

- <mark>You can copy a previous campaign using the Create campaign button in the Campaigns program.</mark> 

- <mark>You can copy a previous campaign based on the entered campaign responses using the Create campaign based on responses button in the Campaigns program.</mark> 

- <mark>You can create a campaign manually by manually selecting companies or contacts to be included.</mark> 

<mark>There is a function to send an e-mail to a campaign recipient. The e-mail address is retrieved from the Contact details table. If there is a contact specified on the campaign row, the individual's e- mail address is retrieved, otherwise the company's e-mail address is retrieved. The e-mail address can be directed to either the address field or the BCC field. If there are documents linked to the campaign in Jeeves Document management, these will be attached to the e-mail.</mark> 

#### 4.5.2 Generate activities 

<mark>There is a function to create planned activities for the salesperson responsible for a campaign recipient. The function warns if there are recipients not connected to a salesperson.</mark> 

#### 4.5.3 Reporting of responses 

<mark>There is a function that simplifies reporting of responses through a checklist and standard replies, and with a link to reported activities.</mark> 

#### 4.5.4 Campaign follow-up 

<mark>When a campaign has been released you can follow up the outcome in the form of number of replies, reported hours, number of opportunities, number of quotations, number of orders and</mark>

Jeeves ERP CRM

<mark>order value. You can also see the corresponding ratios in percentage compared to the number of respondents.</mark> 

### 4.6 Competitor monitoring 

<mark>There are functions for entering information and for monitoring the company's competitors. This refers foremost to information that makes it possible to compare your own company with competitors regarding product assortment, segmentation and financial measurements. Here you also can enter details about previous occasions when you have won or lost a procurement process against a competitor and the key factors that led to the won or lost deal.</mark> 

<mark>In order to simplify the maintenance of the data there is a function to mark when key performance indicators and SWOT categorizations are no longer pertinent. If you check the Obsolete box in any of these rows, the row will not be displayed for the competitor company in the future.</mark> 

### 4.7 Integration with other applications 

<mark>As the module is fully integrated with other applications in Jeeves ERP, you can produce data on customer orders, orders, manufacturing orders, invoices, ledger balances. There are ready-made links that provide a selection of data. You can also create your own program links if you need more detailed information.</mark> 

<mark>There is a global link to Jeeves Document management. You can use the link to connect the documents, e-mail etc. to companies, business opportunities and contacts and thus make information accessible and easy to find.</mark> 

<mark>You can also store templates for your frequently used business documents in Jeeves Document management, to enable automatic creation and storage of documents, like letters or quotations, linked to the current company, or contact. The module can be further linked to external programs such as MS Word and Excel to obtain data from the database and create compilations and letters.</mark> 

<mark>To facilitate field sales there is functionality for replicating contact information between Jeeves ERP and Outlook. This can either be performed via a function on the contact record, copying the current contact to the personal contacts folder in Outlook, or as a service copying all contacts to a public folder in Outlook.</mark>

## 5 Jeeves ERP Purchases 

<mark>Jeeves ERP’s purchasing module can help business enterprises, organizations and authorities improve and fine-tune their purchasing process, irrespective of industry or field.</mark> 

<mark>With Jeeves Purchases you manage the entire purchasing process – from requirement calculations and requisitions, to matching deliveries with supplier invoices and producing supplier statistics and evaluations – within the same application. Communication with external applications, for example, with services for handling eInvoice s from suppliers, is fully integrated and handled from within the Jeeves application.</mark> 

<mark>The main purchasing process and common sub processes are packaged with pre-defined roles, rolespecific menus and forms as well as user guides and training material. Besides these processes, you can activate other, wider functionality for specific company needs.</mark> 

### 5.1 Companies and suppliers 

<mark>In Jeeves ERP, you have a common Company table for company information regardless of the type of company – prospective customers and business opportunities, customers, carriers and suppliers. This eliminates the need for duplicate entries and maintenance of company information.</mark> 

<mark>Additional, supplier-specific information is managed in related supplier tables. Here you can handle all types of suppliers, domestic and international, suppliers with another payee, with factoring etc.</mark> 

#### 5.1.1 Supplier categorization 

<mark>You can handle any number of supplier categories to control approval and posting templates as well as statistics etc. You can have any number of supplier types for supplier evaluations.</mark> 

#### 5.1.2 Language management 

<mark>You can manage the language of item names, units, payment terms, delivery terms, delivery methods, currency clause texts, etc. and print external documents in the supplier's language.</mark> 

### 5.2 Purchased products and services 

<mark>Jeeves ERP also has a common Items table for information regardless of the type of products and items – finished products, semi-manufactured, components, raw materials, packaging, consumables, stocked or non-stocked.</mark> 

#### 5.2.1 Product/item classification 

<mark>There are many possibilities for categorizing and classifying items and products, for example:</mark> 

- <mark>product classes – can control pricing and be used for statistics</mark> 

- <mark>product accounts – control inventory management and valuation, and can control inventory postings</mark> 

- <mark>item groups – can control pricing and be used for statistics and budget allocation</mark> 

- <mark>item classes – can be used for purchase planning, statistics and budget allocation</mark> 

- <mark>item categories – can be used for statistics, for example, for collections of item groups</mark> 

- <mark>item types– can be used for statistics</mark> 

#### 5.2.2 Product variants 

<mark>There is functionality for handling product variants, useful for companies in the fashion industry for example, for defining applicable combinations of say, models, colors, sizes etc. of a product with the help of a matrix. The matrix has various layers that you can switch between for viewing current and planned inventory balances, item numbers, alternative item descriptions, delivery dates, minimum purchase quantities - or if there are none, economic order quantities, prices etc. for each</mark>
